  body.innerHTML=`
    <div class="ethvelle-audio-library-head">
      <button type="button" class="ethvelle-audio-library-back" onclick="returnToAudioPanel()" aria-label="Back to audio options"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6"/></svg></button>
      <div class="ethvelle-audio-library-heading">Audio Library</div>
    </div>
    <div class="editor-asset-toolbar">
      <div class="editor-asset-search-wrap" id="ethvelleAudioSearchWrap">
        <svg class="editor-asset-search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.2 4.2"></path></svg>
        <input class="editor-asset-search" id="ethvelleAudioLibrarySearch" type="search" aria-label="Search audio files" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search audio files…" oninput="filterEthvelleAudioLibrary(this.value)">
        <button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" id="ethvelleAudioSearchClear" onclick="clearEthvelleAudioLibrarySearch()" aria-label="Clear audio search"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div id="ethvelleAudioLibraryResults" class="editor-asset-results"></div>`;
  renderEthvelleAudioLibraryResults();
}
function renderEthvelleAudioLibraryResults(){
  const host=document.getElementById('ethvelleAudioLibraryResults');if(!host)return;
  const query=String(ethvelleAudioLibraryQuery||'').trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
  const audioAssets=getEditorAssets().filter(asset=>editorAssetCategory(asset)==='audio'&&String(asset.src||'').trim());
  const results=audioAssets.filter(asset=>{
    const text=[asset.name,asset.description,asset.keywords,asset.tags,asset.category,'audio'].flat().join(' ').toLocaleLowerCase();
    return !query||text.includes(query);
  });
  if(!results.length){
    host.innerHTML=audioAssets.length
      ? '<div class="ethvelle-audio-library-empty"><b>No matching audio files</b>Try another search keyword.</div>'
      : '<div class="ethvelle-audio-library-empty"><b>No audio files published yet</b>Audio files published under Admin → Assets → Audio will appear here. You can still use Upload to add audio from your device.</div>';
    return;
  }
  host.innerHTML=`<div class="ethvelle-audio-library-list">${results.map(asset=>{
    const id=String(asset.id||'');
    const src=String(asset.src||'');
    const name=String(asset.name||'Audio');
    const duration=Number(asset.duration)||0;
    const meta=duration>0?`Audio file · ${Math.floor(duration/60)}:${String(Math.floor(duration%60)).padStart(2,'0')}`:'Audio file';
    return `<article class="ethvelle-audio-library-card">
      <div class="ethvelle-audio-library-icon" aria-hidden="true">♫</div>
      <div class="ethvelle-audio-library-info">
        <span class="ethvelle-audio-library-name">${escapeHtml(name)}</span>
        <span class="ethvelle-audio-library-meta">${escapeHtml(meta)}</span>
        <audio controls preload="none" src="${escapeHtml(src)}" aria-label="Preview ${escapeHtml(name)}"></audio>
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="ethvelle-audio-library-add" onclick="addAdminAssetToProject('${escapeHtml(id)}')">Add to timeline</button>
    </article>`;
  }).join('')}</div>`;
}

const EDITOR_ASSET_FILTERS=[
  {id:'all',label:'All assets'},
  {id:'image',label:'Image clips'},
  {id:'video',label:'GIF clips'},
  {id:'background-image',label:'Background images'},
  {id:'sticker',label:'Stickers'}
];
function editorAssetCategory(asset){
  const raw=String(asset?.category||asset?.type||'image').toLowerCase();
  if(['background-image','background-video','audio','font','image','video','sticker'].includes(raw))return raw;
  if(raw==='backgroundimage')return 'background-image';
  if(raw==='backgroundvideo')return 'background-video';
  return 'image';
}
function editorAssetLabel(asset){
  const category=editorAssetCategory(asset);
  return ({image:'Image clip / overlay',video:'Video clip', 'background-image':'Background image','background-video':'Background video',audio:'Audio clip',sticker:'Sticker'})[category]||'Asset';
}
function renderAssetsPanelBody(){
  return `<div class="editor-asset-toolbar">
      <div class="editor-asset-search-wrap${editorAssetSearchQuery?' has-query':''}">
        <svg class="editor-asset-search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.2 4.2"></path></svg>
        <input class="editor-asset-search" id="editorAssetSearch" type="search" aria-label="Search assets" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search images, videos, backgrounds, stickers…" value="${escapeHtml(editorAssetSearchQuery)}" oninput="filterEditorAssets(this.value)">
        <button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" id="editorAssetSearchClear" onclick="clearEditorAssetSearch()" aria-label="Clear asset search"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button>
      </div>
      <div class="editor-asset-filters" id="editorAssetFilters">${EDITOR_ASSET_FILTERS.map(f=>`<button type="button" class="editor-asset-filter ${editorAssetCategoryFilter===f.id?'active':''}" data-filter="${f.id}" onclick="setEditorAssetCategory('${f.id}')">${f.label}</button>`).join('')}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="editor-asset-results" id="editorAssetResults"></div>`;
}
function renderAssetsPanel(){
  const el=document.getElementById('editorAssetLibrary');
  if(el){el.innerHTML=renderAssetsPanelBody();renderFilteredEditorAssets();}
}
function syncEditorAssetSearchClear(value){
  const wrap=document.querySelector('#screen-editor .editor-asset-search-wrap');
  if(wrap)wrap.classList.toggle('has-query',!!String(value||'').length);
}
function clearEditorAssetSearch(){
  const input=document.getElementById('editorAssetSearch');
  if(!input)return;
  input.value='';filterEditorAssets('');
  try{input.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(_e){input.focus();}
}
function filterEditorAssets(value){editorAssetSearchQuery=String(value||'');syncEditorAssetSearchClear(editorAssetSearchQuery);renderFilteredEditorAssets();}
function setEditorAssetCategory(category){
  editorAssetCategoryFilter=EDITOR_ASSET_FILTERS.some(f=>f.id===category)?category:'all';
  document.querySelectorAll('#editorAssetFilters .editor-asset-filter').forEach(btn=>btn.classList.toggle('active',btn.dataset.filter===editorAssetCategoryFilter));
  renderFilteredEditorAssets();
}
function renderFilteredEditorAssets(){
  const host=document.getElementById('editorAssetResults');if(!host)return;
  const all=getEditorAssets();
  const q=editorAssetSearchQuery.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
  const assets=all.filter(a=>{
    const category=editorAssetCategory(a);
    if(editorAssetCategoryFilter!=='all'&&category!==editorAssetCategoryFilter)return false;
    const searchable=[a.name,a.description,a.keywords,a.tags,a.fontFamily,category,editorAssetLabel(a)].flat().join(' ').toLocaleLowerCase();
    return !q||searchable.includes(q);
  });
  if(!assets.length){
    host.innerHTML=`<div class="editor-asset-empty">${all.length?'No matching assets found. Try another search or category.':'No admin assets published yet. Assets added in the Admin → Assets section will appear here.'}</div>`;
    return;
  }
  host.innerHTML=`<div class="editor-assets-grid">${assets.map(a=>{
    const category=editorAssetCategory(a),src=String(a.src||'');
    let preview='';
    if(category==='font'){ensureAdminFontLoaded(a);preview=`<div class="editor-asset-font-preview" style="font-family:${escapeHtml(a.fontFamily||a.name||'sans-serif')},sans-serif">Aa</div>`;}
    else if(category==='image'||category==='background-image')preview=src?`<img src="${escapeHtml(src)}" alt="" loading="lazy">`:'IMAGE';
    else if(category==='video'||category==='background-video')preview=`<div class="editor-asset-video">▶ VIDEO</div>`;
    else if(category==='audio')preview=`<div class="editor-asset-font-preview">♫</div>`;
    else preview='MEDIA';
    return `<button type="button" class="editor-asset-card" onclick="addAdminAssetToProject('${escapeHtml(a.id)}')"><span class="editor-asset-thumb">${preview}</span><span class="editor-asset-name">${escapeHtml(a.name||'Asset')}</span><span class="editor-asset-category">${escapeHtml(editorAssetLabel(a))}</span></button>`;
  }).join('')}</div>`;
}
function ensureAdminFontLoaded(assetOrFamily){
  const asset=typeof assetOrFamily==='object'&&assetOrFamily?assetOrFamily:getEditorAssets().find(a=>editorAssetCategory(a)==='font'&&String(a.fontFamily||a.name||'').toLowerCase()===String(assetOrFamily||'').toLowerCase());
  const family=String(asset?.fontFamily||asset?.name||assetOrFamily||'').trim();
  if(!family)return;
  const safeFamily=family.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"');
  if(asset?.src){
    const src=String(asset.src);
    const key='ethvelle-font-'+family.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-');
    if(document.getElementById(key))return;
    if(/\.(woff2?|ttf|otf)(?:[?#].*)?$/i.test(src)){
      const style=document.createElement('style');style.id=key;
      style.textContent='@font-face{font-family:"'+safeFamily+'";src:url("'+src.replace(/"/g,'%22')+'");font-display:swap;}';
      document.head.appendChild(style);
    }else if(/^https:\/\//i.test(src)){
      const link=document.createElement('link');link.id=key;link.rel='stylesheet';link.href=src;document.head.appendChild(link);
    }
  }else{
    const key='ethvelle-font-'+family.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-');
    if(!document.getElementById(key)){
      const link=document.createElement('link');link.id=key;link.rel='stylesheet';
      const bengaliStylesheet=ETHVELLE_BN_WEB_FONT_CSS[family];
      link.href=bengaliStylesheet||('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family='+encodeURIComponent(family).replace(/%20/g,'+')+'&display=swap');
      document.head.appendChild(link);
    }
  }
}
function applyAdminFontAsset(asset){
  const family=String(asset.fontFamily||asset.name||'').trim();if(!family)return;
  ensureAdminFontLoaded(asset);
  const textClip=selectedTextOverlay();
  if(textClip){
    textClip.fontFamily=family;
    renderOverlayLayers();renderFrame();persistProjectState();
    showToast('Font applied to selected text',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1400});
    closePanel();
  }else{
    pendingAdminFontFamily=family;
    openPanel('text');
    showToast('Font selected  -  add text or apply it in the text editor',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800});
  }
}
function addAdminAssetToProject(id){
  const asset=getEditorAssets().find(a=>a.id===id);if(!asset){showToast('This asset is unavailable',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
  const category=editorAssetCategory(asset),src=String(asset.src||'');
  const videoOptions={sourceDuration:Number(asset.sourceDuration)||Number(asset.duration)||5,sourceOffset:Number(asset.sourceTrimStart)||0,adjustments:asset.adjustments||null,chromaKey:asset.chromaKey||null};
  const videoDuration=Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,(Number(asset.sourceTrimEnd)||Number(asset.sourceDuration)||5)-(Number(asset.sourceTrimStart)||0));
  if(category==='font'){applyAdminFontAsset(asset);return;}
  if(!src){showToast('This asset has no media URL',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
  if(!currentProject){showToast('Open a project first',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
  if(category==='background-image'){
    addBackgroundClip(src);closePanel();showToast('Background image added',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1300});return;
  }
  if(category==='background-video'){
    addMediaOverlay({type:'video',src,name:asset.name||'Background video',duration:videoDuration,thumbs:[],peaks:[],...videoOptions});
    const clip=overlays.slice().reverse().find(o=>o.type==='video'&&o.src===src&&(o.name===(asset.name||'Background video')));
    if(clip){const idx=overlays.indexOf(clip);if(idx>0){overlays.splice(idx,1);overlays.unshift(clip);renderOverlayLayers();renderFrame();persistProjectState();}}
    closePanel();showToast('Background video added behind other media',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1400});return;
  }
  if(category==='audio'){
    addMediaOverlay({type:'audio',src,name:asset.name||'Audio',duration:Number(asset.duration)||5,thumbs:[],peaks:[]});
    const addedAudio=[...overlays].reverse().find(o=>o.type==='audio'&&o.src===src&&(o.name===(asset.name||'Audio')));
    if(addedAudio && typeof generateAudioWaveform==='function'){
      generateAudioWaveform(src).then(({peaks})=>{
        if(!overlays.includes(addedAudio))return;
        addedAudio.peaks=Array.from(peaks||[]);
        renderOverlayLayers();
        persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
      }).catch(()=>{});
    }
    closePanel();return;
  }
  const type=(category==='video'||asset.type==='video')?'video':'image';
  addMediaOverlay({type,src,name:asset.name||'Asset',duration:type==='video'?videoDuration:(Number(asset.duration)||5),thumbs:[],peaks:[],...(type==='video'?videoOptions:{})});
  closePanel();
}
function renderPromptGrid(targetId,prompts){
  const wrap=document.getElementById(targetId);if(!wrap)return;
  if(!prompts.length){wrap.innerHTML='<div class="home-prompt-empty">No prompts published yet.</div>';return;}
  wrap.innerHTML=prompts.map(p=>{
    const image=p.templateImage||p.referenceImage;
    const thumb=image?`<img src="${escapeHtml(image)}" alt="${escapeHtml(p.title||'Prompt')}" loading="lazy">`:'PROMPT';
    const imageTypes=[p.referenceImage?'Reference image':'',p.templateImage?'Template image':''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    const desc=p.guidance||p.prompt||'Tap to view and copy this prompt.';
    return `<button class="home-prompt-card" onclick="openPromptDetails('${escapeHtml(p.id)}')"><span class="home-prompt-thumb">${thumb}</span><span class="home-prompt-name">${escapeHtml(p.title||'Prompt')}</span>${imageTypes?`<span class="home-prompt-desc">${escapeHtml(imageTypes)}</span>`:''}<span class="home-prompt-desc">${escapeHtml(desc)}</span></button>`;
  }).join('');
}
let promptSearchQuery='';
function renderPromptLibrary(){
  const allPrompts=getPromptLibrary();
  const q=String(promptSearchQuery||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const prompts=q?allPrompts.filter(p=>[p.title,p.guidance,p.prompt,p.referenceImage?'Reference image':'',p.templateImage?'Template image':''].filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q)):allPrompts;
  const wrap=document.getElementById('keyframePromptGrid');
  if(q && !prompts.length){if(wrap)wrap.innerHTML='<div class="home-prompt-empty">No prompts match your search. Try another keyword.</div>';return;}
  renderPromptGrid('keyframePromptGrid',prompts);
}
function onPromptSearchInput(value){
  promptSearchQuery=String(value||'').trim();
  const box=document.getElementById('promptSearchBox');
  if(box)box.classList.toggle('has-query',!!promptSearchQuery);
  renderPromptLibrary();
}
function clearPromptSearch(){
  promptSearchQuery='';
  const input=document.getElementById('promptSearchInput');
  if(input){input.value='';input.focus();}
  const box=document.getElementById('promptSearchBox');
  if(box)box.classList.remove('has-query');
  renderPromptLibrary();
}
let currentPromptToCopy='';
function openPromptDetails(id){
  const p=getPromptLibrary().find(x=>x.id===id);if(!p)return;currentPromptToCopy=String(p.prompt||'');
  document.getElementById('promptDetailTitle').textContent=p.title||'Prompt';
  const html=[];
  if(p.guidance)html.push(`<div class="prompt-detail-label">Guidance</div><div class="prompt-detail-copy prompt-guidance-copy">${escapeHtml(p.guidance)}</div>`);
  html.push(`<div class="prompt-detail-label">Prompt</div><div class="prompt-detail-copy" id="promptBodyText">${escapeHtml(p.prompt||'')}</div>`);
  const imageDownloadButton=(url,kind,title)=>`<button type="button" class="prompt-image-download" data-src="${escapeHtml(url)}" data-kind="${kind}" data-title="${escapeHtml(title||'Ethvelle') }" aria-label="Download ${kind} image" title="Download image" onclick="event.stopPropagation();downloadPromptImage(this)"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 3v12m-5-5 5 5 5-5M5 16v4h14v-4"/></svg></button>`;
  if(p.referenceImage)html.push(`<div class="prompt-detail-label">Reference image</div><div class="prompt-detail-img-wrap"><img class="prompt-detail-img" src="${escapeHtml(p.referenceImage)}" alt="Reference image">${imageDownloadButton(p.referenceImage,'reference',p.title)}</div>`);
  if(p.templateImage)html.push(`<div class="prompt-detail-label">Template image</div><div class="prompt-detail-img-wrap"><img class="prompt-detail-img" src="${escapeHtml(p.templateImage)}" alt="Template image">${imageDownloadButton(p.templateImage,'template',p.title)}</div>`);
  document.getElementById('promptDetailBody').innerHTML=html.join('');document.getElementById('promptDetailBackdrop').classList.add('active');
  // Make the Prompt block as tall as the Reference image block.
  const refWrap=document.querySelector('#promptDetailBody .prompt-detail-img-wrap'),promptBox=document.getElementById('promptBodyText');
  if(refWrap&&promptBox){
    const syncPromptHeight=()=>{const h=refWrap.offsetHeight;if(h>60){promptBox.style.minHeight=h+'px';promptBox.style.maxHeight=h+'px';}};
    const refImg=refWrap.querySelector('img');
    if(refImg&&!refImg.complete)refImg.addEventListener('load',syncPromptHeight,{once:true});
    syncPromptHeight();
  }
}
async function downloadPromptImage(button){
  if(!button||button.disabled)return;
  const url=button.dataset.src||'';
  if(!url){showToast('Image is unavailable',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
  const kind=button.dataset.kind==='template'?'template':'reference';
  const title=String(button.dataset.title||'Ethvelle').normalize('NFKD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]+/g,'-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g,'').slice(0,55)||'Ethvelle';
  const baseName=`${title}-${kind}-image`;
  const originalText=button.querySelector('span')?.textContent||'Download';
  button.disabled=true;
  if(button.querySelector('span'))button.querySelector('span').textContent='Downloading…';
  try{
    const response=await fetch(url,{mode:'cors',credentials:'omit'});
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error('Image request failed');
    const blob=await response.blob();
    if(!blob.size)throw new Error('Empty image');
    const mime=(blob.type||'').split(';')[0].toLowerCase();
    const mimeExt={'image/jpeg':'jpg','image/jpg':'jpg','image/png':'png','image/webp':'webp','image/gif':'gif','image/avif':'avif','image/bmp':'bmp','image/svg+xml':'svg'};
    let ext=mimeExt[mime]||'';
    if(!ext){try{const path=new URL(url,location.href).pathname;const match=path.match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,5})$/i);ext=match?match[1].toLowerCase():'jpg';}catch(e){ext='jpg';}}
    const objectUrl=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=objectUrl;link.download=`${baseName}.${ext}`;link.style.display='none';
    document.body.appendChild(link);link.click();link.remove();
    setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl),1500);
    showToast('Image downloaded',{icon:toastIcons.check});
  }catch(error){
    // Some image hosts block cross-origin downloads. Open the source directly as a fallback.
    const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=url;link.target='_blank';link.rel='noopener noreferrer';link.download=`${baseName}.jpg`;link.style.display='none';
    document.body.appendChild(link);link.click();link.remove();
    showToast('Opening image  -  use Save image if download does not start',{icon:toastIcons.info});
  }finally{
    button.disabled=false;
    if(button.querySelector('span'))button.querySelector('span').textContent=originalText;
  }
}
function closePromptDetails(){document.getElementById('promptDetailBackdrop').classList.remove('active');}
function copyPromptText(){
  const text=String(currentPromptToCopy||'');if(!text){showToast('No prompt text to copy',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
  if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(()=>showToast('Prompt copied',{icon:toastIcons.check})).catch(()=>fallbackCopyPrompt(text));}
  else fallbackCopyPrompt(text);
}
function fallbackCopyPrompt(text){const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=text;ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();let ok=false;try{ok=document.execCommand('copy');}catch(e){}ta.remove();showToast(ok?'Prompt copied':'Select and copy the prompt manually',{icon:ok?toastIcons.check:toastIcons.info});}

/* Location-based in-app notification popups. Existing inbox notifications remain available too. */
let locationPopupNotificationId=null;
function notifPopupSeenKey(uid){return 'riggy.notifPopupSeen.'+uid;}
function getNotifPopupSeen(){return currentUser?readIdList(notifPopupSeenKey(currentUser.uid)):[];}
function activeScreenId(){const s=document.querySelector('.screen.active');return s?(s.id||'').replace(/^screen-/,''):'';}
function normalizeNotificationScreen(value){
  const v=String(value==null?'':value).trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g,'');
  if(!v) return '';
  if(v==='all'||v==='everywhere'||v==='allscreens') return 'all';
  if(v.startsWith('home')||v.includes('homepage')||v.includes('homescreen')) return 'home';
  if(v.startsWith('project')||v.includes('projectscreen')) return 'projects';
  if(v.startsWith('profile')||v.includes('profilescreen')) return 'profile';
  if(v.startsWith('payment')||v.includes('paymentscreen')) return 'payment';
  return v.replace(/screen$/,'');
}
function notificationShowTargets(value){
  if(Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(normalizeNotificationScreen).filter(Boolean);
  if(typeof value==='string' && value.trim()) return value.split(/[;,|]/).map(normalizeNotificationScreen).filter(Boolean);
  if(value && typeof value==='object') return Object.keys(value).filter(k=>value[k]===true || value[k]===1 || value[k]==='true').map(normalizeNotificationScreen).filter(Boolean);
  return [];
}
function maybeShowLocationNotification(screenName){
  if(!currentUser||!screenName||!Array.isArray(myNotifications))return;
  const active=normalizeNotificationScreen(screenName);
  if(active==='splash'||active==='login'||active==='landing')return; // wait: it will show once the user reaches an app screen
  const backdrop=document.getElementById('notificationPopupBackdrop');if(!backdrop||backdrop.classList.contains('active'))return;
  const titleEl=document.getElementById('notificationPopupTitle'), bodyEl=document.getElementById('notificationPopupBody');
  if(!titleEl||!bodyEl)return;
  const seen=getNotifPopupSeen();
  const candidate=visibleNotifications().find(n=>{
    if(seen.includes(n.id)) return false;
    const rawTarget = n.showOn ?? n.showOnScreens ?? n.popupOn ?? n.displayOn ?? n.screen ?? n.location ?? n.showLocation ?? n.popupLocation ?? n.displayLocation;
    const targets=notificationShowTargets(rawTarget);
    // Older admin notifications omit their placement field; they are treated as Home notifications.
    // "All screens" should be eligible everywhere, regardless of its original casing/spacing.
    return targets.length ? (targets.includes('all') || targets.includes(active)) : active==='home';
  });
  if(!candidate)return;
  locationPopupNotificationId=candidate.id;
  titleEl.textContent=candidate.title||candidate.subject||'Notification';
  bodyEl.textContent=candidate.body||candidate.message||candidate.description||'';
  backdrop.classList.add('active');
  seen.push(candidate.id);writeIdList(notifPopupSeenKey(currentUser.uid),seen);
}
function closeLocationNotification(){const el=document.getElementById('notificationPopupBackdrop');if(el)el.classList.remove('active');locationPopupNotificationId=null;setTimeout(()=>maybeShowLocationNotification(activeScreenId()),180);}
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.visibilityState==='visible')setTimeout(()=>maybeShowLocationNotification(activeScreenId()),220);});
window.addEventListener('focus',()=>setTimeout(()=>maybeShowLocationNotification(activeScreenId()),180));

/* ================= SITE CONTENT (read-only mirror of admin-published data) =================
   Banners / example animations / subscription plans are managed from the separate admin
   panel file (riggy-admin-panel.html), which writes them to Firestore under
   siteContent/main. This app only ever READS that document  -  it has no admin login and
   cannot edit this content. Falls back to localStorage (cached from the last successful
   read) and finally to the built-in defaults below if Firestore isn't reachable, so the
   Home/Payment screens always have something to show. */
const SITE_CONTENT_CACHE_KEY = 'riggy.siteContent.cache.v1';
const SPLIT_SITE_CONTENT_KEYS = ['promoPopups','prompts','assets','models','animationAccess','effects'];
const splitSiteContentLoaded = { promoPopups:false, prompts:false, assets:false, models:false, animationAccess:false, effects:false };
const splitSiteContentExists = { promoPopups:false, prompts:false, assets:false, models:false, animationAccess:false, effects:false };
let splitSiteContentUnsubs = [];
// customAnimPresets: admin-authored entries in the SAME shape as the built-in animPresets
// object below ({ name, duration, keyframes:[{t, pose:{...}}] }), keyed by a slug the admin
// panel generates from the name. Kept as its own array of {key, preset} pairs in Firestore
// (not merged into animPresets directly in storage) so the admin panel can list/edit/delete
// them independently of the built-ins  -  applyCustomAnimPresets() below is what actually
// merges them into the live animPresets object the Animate picker and playback read from.
let siteContent = { banners:null, animations:null, plans:null, tiles:null, tutorials:null, contactInfo:null, creditSettings:null, promoPopups:null, customAnimPresets:null, prompts:[], assets:[], models:[], animationAccess:{}, effects:[] };
try{
  const raw = localStorage.getItem(SITE_CONTENT_CACHE_KEY);
  if(raw) siteContent = JSON.parse(raw);
}catch(e){}
// The earlier app script initializes this list without touching siteContent. Hydrate it only
// after siteContent has been declared and the local cache has been parsed safely.
publishedModelAssets = evPublishedModelsFromSite();

// Tutorials start empty (not a handful of placeholder cards like banners/tiles/animations
// do)  -  until the admin adds real videos from the admin panel's Tutorials page, the screen
